# 当前视觉到控制通路接力记忆

更新时间：2026-07-10

本文用于让新的会话无缝接上当前工作。优先级高于早期过时记录，但不替代设计总文档。当前主要参考仍是：

- `design/全系统分层视觉与中间层协同总体规划.md`
- `design/中间层完整规划.md`
- `design/视觉分层识别与事件驱动更新设计.md`

## 1. 当前总目标

项目目标是把 OpenART 视觉层、RT1064 MCU 协议层、中间层导航/规划层、控制层贯通，最终实现：

```text
OpenART 识别地图和车位姿
  -> UART 发送视觉协议帧
  -> MCU 解析 POS_UPDATE / FULL_MAP
  -> MCU 保存完整视觉状态
  -> 中间层读取视觉状态
  -> 计算导航/推箱子动作
  -> motion_control 控制三轮底盘
```

当前阶段不是完整推箱子，而是先做“最小闭环干运行”：

```text
视觉 pose -> MCU 解析 -> 内部状态缓存 -> 读取 pose/map -> 计算到固定目标点的方向/速度
```

当前 dry-run 只计算命令，不真正驱动车，防止首次闭环时小车乱跑。

## 3. 串口和接线约定

MCU 调试/逐飞助手串口：

```c
DEBUG_UART_INDEX    = UART_8
DEBUG_UART_BAUDRATE = 115200
DEBUG_UART_TX_PIN   = UART8_TX_D16
DEBUG_UART_RX_PIN   = UART8_RX_D17
```

MCU 与 OpenART 视觉通信：

```text
MCU: UART4 C16/C17, 115200
OpenART: 多 UART 广播，当前尝试 UART5 / UART12 / UART11
```

实际接线原则：

```text
OpenART TX -> MCU C17/RX
OpenART RX -> MCU C16/TX
GND 共地
```

当前 MCU 端视觉串口宏在 `vision_link.c`：

```c
#define VISION_LINK_UART_INDEX   (UART_4)
#define VISION_LINK_UART_TX_PIN  (UART4_TX_C16)
#define VISION_LINK_UART_RX_PIN  (UART4_RX_C17)
```

## 4. 版本识别约定

每次结束必须告诉用户：

```text
MCU端：是否更新，当前 id 是多少，是否需要重新下载 axf
视觉端：是否更新，当前 oa_id 是多少，是否已同步到 G:\
```

当前版本：

```text
MCU端：id=7100805
视觉端：oa_id=7100410
```

MCU 串口输出应出现：

```text
VIS id=7100805 ...
```

OpenART 终端输出应出现：

```text
OPENART oa_id=7100410 LINK_READY
oa_id=7100410 FPS ...
```

如果逐飞助手里看不到当前 MCU `id`，不要继续分析数据，先确认是否下载了正确的 `Objects\rt1064.axf`。

## 5. 已经验证通过的内容

### 5.1 OpenART 到 MCU 的基础通信

已验证：

```text
online=1
rx 持续增加
pos 持续增加
map 持续增加
err=0/0
drop=0
poll=0
```

含义：

- POS_UPDATE 位置帧可以高频稳定传输。
- FULL_MAP 全图帧可以到达 MCU。
- 校验和和格式解析当前无错误。
- UART 轮询兜底已关闭，仅用中断收字节，避免轮询和中断双重读取扰乱长帧。

关键配置：

```c
#define VISION_LINK_USE_UART_POLL (0U)
```

### 5.2 坐标单位修正

OpenART 发给 MCU 的车位置目前是“格坐标 x10”，例如：

```text
OpenART: CAR (2.2,7.4)
MCU:     car=(2.2,7.4)
```

之前 MCU 串口显示曾把它当成 mm 又除以 200，导致错误显示为 `car=(0.1,0.3)`。这个已修正。

注意：代码内部历史字段名仍有 `car_x_mm/car_y_mm`，但当前实际含义是 `car_x10/car_y10`。后续重构应改名，避免再次误用。

### 5.3 干运行导航计算

当前 MCU 输出里新增：

```text
nav=DRY
tgt=(2.0,6.0)
cmd=(角度,速度)
```

含义：

- MCU 已经从视觉 pose 读取位置。
- MCU 已经计算从当前位置到固定目标点 `(2.0, 6.0)` 的方向和速度。
- 当前只是 dry-run，不调用 `motion_set_velocity()` 驱动车。

例子：

```text
car=(0.4,3.7)
tgt=(2.0,6.0)
cmd=(55.2,220)
```

这说明从 `(0.4,3.7)` 指向 `(2.0,6.0)` 的方向约为 55.2 度，速度命令为 220。

## 6. 当前最新卡点

最新一次用户日志显示：

```text
VIS id=7100410 LINK_OK online=1 ...
pos 持续增加
map/ver 持续增加
err=0/0
nav=DRY
box=0 goal=0 bomb=0 wall=0
```

这说明：

- 通信是通的。
- FULL_MAP 帧被 MCU 收到并解析了。
- 导航干运行链路能跑。
- 但 FULL_MAP 内容像是空地图：墙、目标、箱子、炸弹全为 0。

因此最新 MCU 版本 `id=7100805` 增加了更明确的诊断：

```text
LINK_OK / LINK_WAIT
MAP_OK / MAP_EMPTY
```

当前判定逻辑：

```text
LINK_OK    = 在线、有位置帧、无校验/格式错误
MAP_OK     = 收到全图、尺寸为 16x12、且 wall/goal/box/bomb 至少一类非零
MAP_EMPTY  = 收到或未收到地图，但内容不足以作为有效地图
```

下一步用户需要下载 `id=7100805` 版本，然后看输出是 `MAP_OK` 还是 `MAP_EMPTY`。

## 7. 当前 MCU 代码思路

### 7.1 `vision_link.c/h`

职责：

```text
UART4 接收字节
  -> ring buffer
  -> 解析协议帧
  -> 保存视觉状态
  -> 提供查询接口
```

已新增的数据结构：

```c
vision_link_cell_t
vision_link_map_t
```

已新增/扩展状态：

```text
map_version
map_valid
map_width/map_height
wall_bits[24]
boxes[4]
goals[4]
bombs[2]
```

已新增查询接口：

```c
uint8 vision_link_get_pose_grid(float *x_grid, float *y_grid, float *theta_deg);
uint8 vision_link_get_map(vision_link_map_t *out);
```

FULL_MAP payload 布局：

```text
payload[0]      = GRID_W
payload[1]      = GRID_H
payload[2..25]  = wall bitmap 24B
payload[26]     = box count
payload[27..34] = boxes 4 * (gx, gy)
payload[35]     = goal count
payload[36..43] = goals 4 * (gx, gy)
payload[44]     = bomb count
payload[45..48] = bombs 2 * (gx, gy)
payload[49..50] = car x10, int16 little endian
payload[51..52] = car y10, int16 little endian
payload[53..54] = theta x10, int16 little endian
payload[55]     = xor checksum
```

POS_UPDATE payload 当前固定 19 字节：

```text
payload[0]      = frame id
payload[1..2]   = car x10
payload[3..4]   = car y10
payload[5..6]   = theta x10
payload[7]      = box count
payload[8..]    = box coords
then bomb count and bomb coords
padding to 18 bytes
payload[18]     = xor checksum
```

### 7.2 `app_status.c`

当前用于向逐飞助手输出诊断信息，1 秒左右一次。

当前重要输出格式：

```text
VIS id=7100805 LINK_OK/MAP_EMPTY online=... pos=... map=... ver=...
car=(x,y) theta=...
box=... goal=... bomb=... wall=...
nav=DRY tgt=(2.0,6.0) cmd=(angle,speed)
```

这里的 `nav=DRY` 是干运行，不会驱动车。

### 7.3 `main.c`

当前主循环仍是：

```text
ai_tuner_poll_command()
vision_link_poll()
pentagram_run_task()
app_status_send_vofa()
```

注意：`pentagram_enable = 1` 仍然存在，说明当前控制目标仍主要来自五角星测试逻辑。视觉导航还没有接管运动控制。

### 7.4 `isr.c`

PIT 中断：

```c
motor_control_5ms();
```

LPUART4 中断：

```c
uint8 dat = uart_read_byte(UART_4);
vision_link_rx_byte(dat);
```

当前尚未把 `vision_link_poll()` 放进 PIT 中断，仍在 main while 中解析环形缓冲。

## 8. 当前 OpenART 代码思路

文件：

```text
openart\新视觉main.py
openart\main.py
G:\main.py
G:\boot.py
```

当前 OpenART 关键点：

- VGA 分辨率保持不动。
- 多 UART 广播，候选 `(5, 12, 11)`。
- POS_UPDATE 高频发送，约 20ms 一次。
- FULL_MAP 初始化和周期刷新发送。
- `oa_id=7100410`。
- 启动打印：

```text
OPENART oa_id=7100410 LINK_READY
```

当前未改视觉识别算法，只改了版本标记。最新问题很可能需要回到 OpenART 端确认 `build_current_map(img)` 的输出为什么在当前画面下变成空地图，或者确认当前场地画面是不是没有放地图/阈值没有识别。

## 9. 操作约定

1. 每次改代码前先备份。

备份目录统一放：

```text
backup\
```

命名建议：

```text
backup\mcu_xxx_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS
backup\openart_xxx_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS
backup\full_path_xxx_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS
```

2. 每次改 MCU 输出都换新的 `id=`。

3. 每次改 OpenART 输出都换新的 `oa_id=`。

4. 每次结束必须说明：

```text
MCU端：是否更新，当前 id，是否需要下载 axf
视觉端：是否更新，当前 oa_id，是否已同步 G:\
```

5. 不要让第一次闭环直接动车。先 `nav=DRY`，确认方向、速度、目标点、坐标系都正确，再打开实际运动。

6. 不要把早期旧项目里的大量 VOFA/调试输出直接搬回来。逐飞助手文本输出保持简短、可识别。

7. 当前三份重复/交错的串口显示现象不代表 MCU 协议解析错误。以 `err=0/0`、`drop=0`、`pos/map` 是否增长为准。

8. `raw=` 目前只是早期抓取的前 8 个字节，不应过度解读。

## 10. 下一步建议

### 第一步：验证 `id=7100805`

用户下载最新 MCU AXF：

```text
project\mdk\Objects\rt1064.axf
```

看逐飞助手是否出现：

```text
VIS id=7100805
```

然后看：

```text
LINK_OK MAP_OK
```

或：

```text
LINK_OK MAP_EMPTY
```

### 第二步：如果是 `MAP_EMPTY`

不要先改 MCU 协议，因为通信和帧解析已通。优先查 OpenART：

- OpenART 终端是否为 `oa_id=7100410 LINK_READY`
- OpenART 当前画面是否真有地图
- `build_current_map(img)` 是否输出了全空
- 是否要临时打开 `DEBUG_PRINT_MAP_ON_FULL` 或打印 `boxes/goals/bombs/walls` 计数
- 是否阈值/光照/ROI 导致墙和目标识别不到

建议给 OpenART 也加一行低频地图摘要：

```text
OA_MAP wall=.. box=.. goal=.. bomb=..
```

这样可判断空地图是在 OpenART 生成阶段就空，还是发送/解析阶段变空。

### 第三步：如果是 `MAP_OK`

继续做最小闭环：

1. 观察 `nav=DRY` 的 `cmd=(angle,speed)` 是否符合实际目标方向。
2. 固定小车不动，改变目标点或移动小车，确认角度变化正确。
3. 增加 `VISION_NAV_RUN_ENABLE` 宏，默认 0。
4. 用户确认后改为 1，让 MCU 调用：

```c
motion_set_velocity(cmd_angle, cmd_speed);
```

5. 视觉掉线或 `MAP_EMPTY` 时必须：

```c
motion_fast_brake();
```

### 第四步：再接完整中间层

只有最小闭环能稳定移动车后，才接完整规划：

```text
FULL_MAP -> map_input_t -> solve() -> action_t[] -> path_follower -> motion_control
```

当前副本项目里没有 `vision_parser.c/solver.c/path_follower.c` 这些完整模块，不能假设已经存在。需要后续决定：

- 从其他项目移植这些模块；
- 或者先在当前项目中新建轻量版本；
- 或者仅保留当前 `vision_link`，在它上面逐步扩展。

当前最新版本：

```text
视觉端：oa_id=7100411
备份：backup\bomb_threshold_protocol_20260711_154844
```

```text
G:\main.py
```

  - `car_y` 从 payload[51] 改为 payload[55]
  - `theta` 从 payload[53] 改为 payload[57]

## 14. 2026-07-11 MCU 端 5 秒全图输出

用户希望在逐飞助手串行终端中直接查看 MCU 收到并解析后的全图。已在 MCU 端 `project\code\app_status.c` 增加 5 秒一次的地图文本输出。

当前最新版本：

```text
MCU端：id=7100812
视觉端：oa_id=7100411（本次未改）
备份：backup\mcu_map_dump_5s_20260711_160644
```

输出格式示例：

```text
VIS id=7100812 ...
MAP id=7100812 ver=2 size=16x12 box=3 goal=3 bomb=3 wall=88 car=(0.9,5.9) theta=0.0
MAP00 ################
MAP01 #...#
...
MAP11 ################
MAP_END
```

地图重建规则：

- 先按 `wall_bits` 填 `#` / `-`
- 再叠加目标 `.`
- 再叠加箱子 `$`
- 再叠加炸弹 `*`
- 不在地图里叠加车，车位置只放在 `MAP id=...` 头行中

验证：

```text
Keil build: 0 Error(s), 0 Warning(s)
AXF: project\mdk\Objects\rt1064.axf
AXF 内确认包含 VIS id=7100812 和 MAP id=7100812，且不再包含 7100811
```

## 16. 常用测试地图 map3

用户说明后续会经常使用这张地图：

```text
C:\Users\L\Desktop\SmartCar_VR_V1.4\map_file\map3.txt
```

内容：

```text
################
#-#---#--#----.#
#---#--#-####--#
##-###-#-#-----#
#-.#-#-#-#--#--#
#-##-#---------#
#--#-------##--#
#-*-------*-#--#
#--$-#-----$*--#
#-$--########--#
#----#.--------#
################
```

理论统计：

```text
wall # = 89
empty - = 94
goal . = 3
box  $ = 3
bomb * = 3
```

关键坐标：

```text
目标点 . : (14,1) (2,4) (6,10)
箱子   $ : (3,8)  (11,8) (2,9)
炸弹   * : (2,7)  (10,7) (12,8)
```

最近一次 MCU 日志 `VIS id=7100813` 显示：

```text
box=3 goal=3 bomb=3 wall=87 -> 88
car=(1.0~1.1,6.0) theta=0.0/3.8
err=0/0 drop=0 LINK_OK MAP_OK
```

因此链路和特殊元素数量正确，墙仍比 map3 理论值少 1~2 个。要定位具体错格，需要 OpenMV IDE 中视觉端 `OA_MAP oa_id=7100412` 的 `OA00..OA11` 输出。

## 19. 2026-07-11 视觉端 UART 提前初始化诊断

用户继续测试发现车上电源直接冷启动时：

```text
VIS id=7100815 LINK_WAIT MAP_EMPTY
rx=0 pos=0 map=0
req 持续增加
```

含义：MCU 请求在发，但 MCU 视觉串口没有收到摄像头任何字节，连 POS_UPDATE 都没有。因此问题不在 FULL_MAP 协议，而是车上电源启动时摄像头脚本/UART 没有开始对 MCU 发数据，或者 sensor 初始化阶段卡在 UART 初始化之前。

检查代码发现视觉端原顺序是：

```text
sensor.reset()/sensor 初始化
然后 CMM/UART 初始化
然后进入主循环
```

如果车上电源导致 sensor 初始化异常，UART 根本不会打开，MCU 就会一直 `rx=0`。

本次只改视觉端：

```text
视觉端：oa_id=7100414
MCU端：id=7100815（本次未改）
备份：backup\openart_uart_before_sensor_20260711_171614
```

改动：

- 将 CMM/UART 初始化提前到 sensor 初始化之前。
- UART 打开后立即向所有候选 UART 发送 3 次文本心跳：

```text
OA_BOOT_UART oa_id=7100414
```

- sensor 初始化完成后再发送：

```text
OA_BOOT_SENSOR oa_id=7100414
```

- 明确恢复 `sensor.skip_frames(time=300)`，避免它被注释文本吞掉。
- 已同步到：

```text
openart\新视觉main.py
openart\main.py
G:\main.py
G:\boot.py
```

测试判定：

- 如果车上电源冷启动后 MCU 的 `rx` 从 0 变成非 0，但仍没有 `LINK_OK`，说明摄像头脚本至少跑到 UART 初始化，后续可能卡在 sensor 初始化/识别阶段。
- 如果车上电源冷启动后 MCU 仍然 `rx=0`，说明摄像头板没有运行到 UART 初始化，优先查车上电源、电压、地、启动模式、UART 线和供电路径。

## 20. 2026-07-11 OpenMV bytearray 删除兼容性修复

用户发现 OpenMV IDE 第一次运行脚本时弹窗：

```text
TypeError: 'bytearray' object doesn't support item deletion
```

原因定位：

视觉端 `poll_map_request()` 中使用了：

```python
del rx_buf[0]
del rx_buf[:5]
```

当前 OpenMV/MicroPython 版本不支持对 `bytearray` 做 item/slice deletion。一旦 MCU 请求包或串口残留字节进入 `rx_buf`，脚本会直接异常退出。脚本退出后 MCU 自然一直 `rx=0`、`LINK_WAIT MAP_EMPTY`。第一次运行更容易遇到残留字节；第二次缓冲变干净，所以可能“关掉弹窗再运行就好了”。

本次只改视觉端：

```text
视觉端：oa_id=7100415
MCU端：id=7100815（本次未改）
备份：backup\openart_bytearray_rxfix_20260711_172045
```

修复方式：

```python
rx_buf = bytearray(rx_buf[1:])
rx_buf = bytearray(rx_buf[5:])
rx_buf = bytearray(rx_buf[-16:])
```

替代所有 `del rx_buf[...]`。

验证：

```text
python -m py_compile 通过
openart\新视觉main.py / openart\main.py / G:\main.py / G:\boot.py 哈希一致
rg 确认不再存在 del rx_buf
```

测试预期：

- OpenMV IDE 第一次运行不应再弹 `bytearray` 删除错误。
- 车上电源冷启动时，如果脚本能启动，MCU 应至少收到 `OA_BOOT_UART oa_id=7100415` 文本字节，使 `rx` 不再一直为 0。

## 22. 2026-07-11 去除 OpenART 文件 BOM

用户反馈回退后仍无法自启动。检查发现：

```text
G:\boot.py
G:\main.py
openart\新视觉main.py
openart\main.py
```

四个文件开头均为：

```text
EF BB BF 69 6D 70 6F 72
```

即带 UTF-8 BOM。OpenMV/MicroPython 自启动执行 `boot.py/main.py` 时可能不兼容 BOM，导致 USB 直接运行/自启动行为异常。

本次只修编码，不改逻辑：

```text
备份：backup\remove_bom_autostart_20260711_174207
视觉端版本仍为：oa_id=7100417
```

处理后：

```text
G:\boot.py
G:\main.py
openart\新视觉main.py
openart\main.py
```

四份文件：

- SHA256 一致
- 开头为 `69 6D 70 6F 72 74 20 73`，即 `import s`
- `HasBom=False`
- `python -m py_compile` 通过

测试预期：

- 摄像头重新断电再插 USB，应能恢复离线自启动。
- 如果仍不行，下一步优先考虑恢复到更早的备份版本，而不是继续叠启动逻辑。

### 22.1 结论确认

用户随后验证：去除 BOM 后，直接开电源冷启动已经可以工作。

关键日志：

```text
VIS id=7100815 LINK_OK MAP_OK online=1
rx=908 -> 2010 -> 3112 -> 4185
pos=29 -> 67 -> 105 -> 142
map=1 ver=1 req=1 err=0/0 drop=0
```

说明：

- MCU 已收到摄像头数据，`rx` 持续增长。
- 位置帧已持续解析，`pos` 持续增长。
- 已收到一帧全图，`map=1 ver=1`。
- 校验和格式错误均为 0。
- 当前 `box=0 goal=0 bomb=0 wall=19` 是因为测试时没有放正式地图/手机，图像内容乱，不代表链路异常。

因此可以确认：此前“无法自启动/冷启动无输出”的直接原因就是 Python 文件带 UTF-8 BOM。后续所有写入 `G:\main.py` / `G:\boot.py` 的操作必须保证 UTF-8 无 BOM。

```text
Right-front motor:
  DIR pin = C11
  PWM pin = PWM2_MODULE2_CHA_C10
  forward/positive direction level = 1
```

Current normal closed-loop table in `project\code\motion_control.c` should keep:

```c
{QTIMER1_ENCODER2, -1, PWM2_MODULE0_CHA_C6,  C7,  1}, // rear
{QTIMER2_ENCODER1, -1, PWM2_MODULE1_CHA_C8,  C9,  0}, // left front
{QTIMER2_ENCODER2, -1, PWM2_MODULE2_CHA_C10, C11, 1}, // right front
```

Do not change the left-front `forward_level` back to 1. If a future raw motor test uses fixed direction levels, it must use `C7=1, C9=0, C11=1` for all wheels to spin in the same positive direction.

## 29. 2026-07-12 Four-Mode Motor/Encoder/Control Verification

A four-mode verification harness was added. Change only these macros in `project\user\src\main.c`:

```c
#define CONTROL_VERIFY_MODE     (1U)
#define CONTROL_VERIFY_MOTOR    (0U)
#define CONTROL_VERIFY_PWM      (2000U)
```

Mode definitions:

```text
Mode 1: one physical motor open-loop; CONTROL_VERIFY_MOTOR selects 0 rear, 1 left-front, 2 right-front.
Mode 2: all three physical motors receive their verified positive open-loop direction and PWM 2000.
Mode 3: position hold at the boot encoder position using the existing position/speed control logic.
Mode 4: stationary yaw hold at the boot heading using the existing IMU yaw gains, kinematics, and speed PIDs.
```

Physical labels shown on screen are authoritative:

```text
REAR  = motor C10/C11, encoder C2/C24
LEFT  = motor C8/C9,   encoder C5/C25
RIGHT = motor C6/C7,   encoder C0/C1
```

Screen fields:

```text
Modes 1/2: physical wheel name, encoder pins, RAW encoder count, PWM and selected motor/direction levels.
Mode 3: ERR=position target minus actual, SPD=sign-corrected encoder speed, DUTY=final motor PWM.
Mode 4: TGT/YAW/ERR/GYR/ROT CORR plus each wheel's TGT/SPD/DUTY.
```

Isolation and safety:

- Modes 1/2 stop before PIT starts, so no PID output can compete with open-loop PWM.
- Modes 3/4 bypass normal vision/navigation/main-loop commands while the 5 ms PIT control remains active.
- Mode 4 bypasses only the normal low-translation-speed yaw-disable condition; it reuses the existing `YAW_HOLD_KP`, `YAW_HOLD_KD`, and output limit unchanged.
- Modes 3/4 reuse the existing PWM safety limit of 2500.
- All PID values, kinematic angles, encoder signs, physical mapping, and motor forward levels were verified unchanged against the pre-edit backup.

Build and version:

```text
MCU id: 7100826
Default build: mode 1, motor 0 (physical rear)
Backup: backup\mcu_four_mode_control_verify_20260712_015501
Modes 1, 2, 3, and 4 were each built separately: 0 Error(s), 0 Warning(s)
Final AXF was rebuilt with mode 1: project\mdk\Objects\rt1064.axf
Vision side unchanged: oa_id=7100417
```

## 25. 2026-07-12 MCU Wheel Speed Loop Feedback Test

User requested a direct low-speed closed-loop test for all three wheels to check whether any encoder/PID feedback is positive feedback.

Current MCU test version:

```text
MCU id: 7100822
Boot tag: #WHEEL_SPEED_LOOP_TEST target=100
Backup before this edit: backup\mcu_wheel_speed_loop_test_20260712_000427
Vision side unchanged: oa_id=7100417
AXF: project\mdk\Objects\rt1064.axf
Keil build: 0 Error(s), 0 Warning(s)
```

Important behavior:

```text
WHEEL_SPEED_LOOP_TEST_ENABLE = 1
WHEEL_SPEED_LOOP_TEST_TARGET = 100
WHEEL_SPEED_LOOP_TEST_DUTY_LIMIT = 2500
pentagram_enable = 0
RAW_MOTOR_LF_DIR0_TEST = 0
```

Implementation detail:
- `motor_control_5ms()` still reads encoders and updates IMU/odometry first.
- Before normal kinematics/position loop, it injects direct wheel targets:
  `wheel_target_speed[0..2] = 100`.
- It sets `motor*_speed_pid.Target` directly from `wheel_target_speed`.
- It sets `motor*_speed_pid.Actual` from `encoder_speed`.
- It runs only the speed PID and then clamps final `motor_output_duty[]` to `+-2500`.
- It calls `p_set(0..2, duty)` and returns, bypassing pentagram, visual navigation, kinematics, yaw hold, and position compensation.

Expected physical test:
- Download `Objects\rt1064.axf`.
- On boot, serial should include `VIS id=7100822` and `#WHEEL_SPEED_LOOP_TEST target=100`.
- All three wheels should try to run slowly in the positive/forward direction.
- If one wheel suddenly accelerates/fights harder instead of stabilizing, suspect that wheel's encoder feedback sign or motor direction mapping.
- The verified motor direction mapping from section 24 must remain:
  `C7=1, C9=0, C11=1`.

```text
1 = left-front motor, DIR C9, PWM PWM2_MODULE1_CHA_C8
2 = right-front motor, DIR C11, PWM PWM2_MODULE2_CHA_C10
```

Important behavior:
- It uses open-loop PWM, not PID speed loop.
- It enters the test loop before PIT is started, so `motor_control_5ms()` does not run.
- It sets motor direction levels to the verified positive direction: `C7=1, C9=0, C11=1`.
- Only the selected motor gets PWM `+2000`; the other two motors stay at PWM 0.
- Every 100 ms it reads and clears the three currently used encoders.
- IPS screen shows:
  `MOTOR`, `PWM`, `ENC0 C2/C24`, `ENC1 C5/C25`, `ENC2 C0/C1`.
- Serial also prints the same data as:
  `ENC_MAP id=7100824 motor=(0U) pwm=(2000U) enc0=... enc1=... enc2=...`

Current encoder IDs used for observation:

```text
ENC0 = QTIMER1_ENCODER2 = C2/C24
ENC1 = QTIMER2_ENCODER2 = C5/C25
ENC2 = QTIMER1_ENCODER1 = C0/C1
```

User should test motor 0, 1, and 2 one by one and report which ENC changes and with what sign. Then update `wheel_hw[]` encoder order and `encoder_sign`.

## 28. 2026-07-12 Final Physical Wheel/PID Mapping (Supersedes Sections 26-27)

The open-loop identification test is complete. The user's physical observations are authoritative for pins, encoder pairing, and direction. The older verified straight-line project is used only to preserve each PID parameter set with the same physical wheel position.

Measured physical mapping with the current vehicle wiring:

```text
PWM C6 / DIR C7   -> physical right-front wheel -> encoder C0/C1  -> raw count about -7000
PWM C8 / DIR C9   -> physical left-front wheel  -> encoder C5/C25 -> raw count about -7000
PWM C10 / DIR C11 -> physical rear wheel        -> encoder C2/C24 -> raw count about -7000
```

Final control-index mapping in `project\code\motion_control.c`:

```text
wheel0 = rear        angle -90 deg  PID0 (speed Kp=18) encoder C2/C24  sign -1 motor C10/C11 forward_level 1
wheel1 = left-front  angle  30 deg  PID1 (speed Kp=18) encoder C5/C25  sign -1 motor C8/C9   forward_level 0
wheel2 = right-front angle 150 deg  PID2 (speed Kp=20) encoder C0/C1   sign -1 motor C6/C7   forward_level 1
```

Encoder-sign proof from the actual code path:

```text
positive motor command -> raw encoder count is negative
encoder_speed = encoder_sign * raw_count
encoder_sign = -1 -> encoder_speed is positive
PID error = Target - Actual
therefore increasing positive wheel speed reduces positive error: negative feedback
```

Strict rule for future edits:

- Never restore motor/encoder pins from comments in the old project.
- Never move a PID parameter set by electrical pin number.
- PID0 must remain with the physical rear wheel, PID1 with physical left-front, and PID2 with physical right-front.
- Current wiring and measured signs always override old wiring comments.
- Do not change PID gains, limits, control periods, kinematic angles, or direction levels unless the user explicitly requests tuning.

## 45. Dedicated UART8 D16/D17 receive diagnostic (2026-07-15)

MCU build ID: `7100840`. Visual build remains `oa_id=7100418`.

This build replaces the temporary all-UART monitor with the exact debug/wireless
UART wiring used on the car:

```text
UART index: UART8
baud:       115200
MCU TX:     D16
MCU RX:     D17
RTS:        D26
```

Implementation:

```text
1. main.c calls wireless_uart_init(), matching the old known-working project.
2. LPUART8_IRQHandler calls pc_console_uart8_rx_isr() directly.
3. The ISR drains real UART8 bytes into a dedicated 128-byte SPSC ring buffer.
4. The main loop consumes that ring and also directly polls UART8 as fallback.
5. The flawed debug_interrupr_handler/debug FIFO receive path is no longer used.
6. Other UART monitors are removed; display B2/B3 ownership is restored.
```

Expected output after PC sends ASCII `1`:

```text
UART8_RX id=7100840 tx=D16 rx=D17 src=isr hex=31 ascii='1'
```

If the interrupt path fails but the hardware byte reaches UART8:

```text
UART8_RX id=7100840 tx=D16 rx=D17 src=poll hex=31 ascii='1'
```

If neither line appears, the PC/wireless-module TX signal is not reaching MCU
D17; menu parsing and the camera link are not responsible.


### Planned adaptation of the `1.1 上赛道test` middle layer

Use the old project as an algorithm source, not as a whole-project replacement. Preserve the current verified `vision_link`, motor/encoder pin mapping, encoder signs, PID-to-physical-wheel mapping, and motion control implementation.

Integration order:

```text
Stage A: PC menu + manual map request/print (current stage, complete in code)
Stage B: vision_world adapter and immutable map snapshot
Stage C: solver workspace + summary/detail action printing, still no movement
Stage D: mission_manager with RUN_ALL and STEP_WAIT states
Stage E: path_follower adapter to the verified current motion_control
Stage F: controlled low-speed track test, then recovery/replan behavior
Stage G: bomb-specific planning completion and performance/memory optimization
```

Mandatory adaptations before reusing old modules:

```text
1. Coordinates: current visual position is grid coordinate x10. Cell center (m,n) is exactly (m,n), so world conversion is grid * 200 mm, not grid * 200 + 100 mm.
2. Object identity: row-major box/bomb lists are observations, not stable IDs. Add MCU-side nearest-permutation reassociation before a follower relies on box_id.
3. Control API: do not copy the old motor pins, encoder pins, signs, PID assignment, or old motion_control. Add a narrow world/body command adapter around the verified current controller.
4. Heading hold: speed zero between actions must still hold the chosen yaw, matching the verified pentagram behavior.
5. Memory: replace large by-value solver_output_t copies and stack-heavy preprocess objects with static workspaces and pointer-based ownership.
6. Timing: solve only in the main loop. PIT remains bounded to sensor/control/follower updates.
7. Bombs: ACTION_PUSH_BOMB exists in the old type system but is not a complete contest-ready behavior. Box solving should be validated independently before bomb planning is enabled.
8. State machine: SAFE_IDLE, WAIT_MAP, MAP_READY, SOLVING, PLAN_READY, RUN_ALL, STEP_WAIT, ACTION_RUNNING, DONE, FAULT. No transition may start motion without an explicit PC run command.
```

```text
- 有 PC_RX 但 FULL_MAP timeout：MCU 已收到菜单命令，问题转向 MCU->摄像头请求或摄像头->MCU地图帧。
```

## 43. PC 输入无回显的诊断与轮询兜底（2026-07-15）

现象：PC 串口可以看到 MCU 输出，但 PC 向 MCU 发送文本没有任何 `PC_RX` 回传。

代码判断：

```text
原 7100837 只读 debug_read_ring_buffer()。
这个 FIFO 依赖 LPUART8_IRQHandler -> debug_interrupr_handler()。
如果 UART8 RX 中断没有触发，或者 PC TX 没有接到 MCU D17，即使 MCU->PC 输出正常，也不会有回显。
```

MCU 更新为 `id=7100838`：

```text
project/code/pc_console.c
project/user/src/main.c
```

改动：

```text
pc_console_poll_input() 现在先读 debug IRQ FIFO，再直接 uart_query_byte(DEBUG_UART_INDEX) 轮询 UART8。
回显格式增加来源：
PC_RX id=7100838 src=irq  hex=31 ascii='1'
PC_RX id=7100838 src=poll hex=31 ascii='1'
```

判读：

```text
src=irq  : UART8 RX 中断链路正常。
src=poll : 字节确实进了 UART8，但 debug FIFO/中断链路没吃到。
无 PC_RX : PC->MCU 物理输入方向没进来，重点查 PC TX -> MCU D17、GND、发送端口/发送按钮/是否真发。
```

## 46. VERIFIED wireless PC UART baseline - do not change casually (2026-07-15)

Status: **confirmed working on the real car by the user** with MCU build
`7100840`. This section supersedes the failed/temporary receive experiments in
sections 42-45. Future work must preserve this configuration unless the user
explicitly requests a hardware change.

### Physical link and serial format

```text
MCU peripheral:       UART8
baud:                 115200
data bits:            8
parity:               none
stop bits:            1
PC software flow:     none
MCU TX pin:           D16 (UART8_TX_D16)
MCU RX pin:           D17 (UART8_RX_D17)
wireless RTS pin:     D26
common ground:        required
PC payload mode:      text/ASCII, not HEX (for example '1' is byte 0x31)
```

Physical signal direction:

```text
MCU D16 TX  ---> wireless module RX ---> PC receive window
PC transmit ---> wireless module TX ---> MCU D17 RX
wireless module RTS -------------------> MCU D26
```

### Authoritative macros

The debug UART settings in
`libraries/zf_common/zf_common_debug.h` are authoritative:

```c
#define DEBUG_UART_INDEX       (UART_8)
#define DEBUG_UART_BAUDRATE    (115200)
#define DEBUG_UART_TX_PIN      (UART8_TX_D16)
#define DEBUG_UART_RX_PIN      (UART8_RX_D17)
#define DEBUG_UART_USE_INTERRUPT (1)
```

The wireless-module driver in
`libraries/zf_device/zf_device_wireless_uart.h` uses names from the MODULE
point of view. This is the main naming trap:

```c
#define WIRELESS_UART_INDEX    (UART_8)
#define WIRELESS_UART_BUAD_RATE (115200)
#define WIRELESS_UART_TX_PIN   (UART8_RX_D17)  // module TX -> MCU RX
#define WIRELESS_UART_RX_PIN   (UART8_TX_D16)  // module RX <- MCU TX
#define WIRELESS_UART_RTS_PIN  (D26)
```

Do not swap D16 and D17 based on the wireless macro names. Also do not follow
the stale `DEBUG_UART_INDEX UART_1` definition in `project/user/user_config.h`;
the compiled and verified debug configuration is UART8 from
`zf_common_debug.h`.

### Required initialization and receive path

Verified initialization sequence in `project/user/src/main.c`:

```text
clock_init(...)
debug_init()             -> brings up UART8 D16/D17 for debug output
basic safe/motor setup
wireless_uart_init()     -> reinitializes UART8 at 115200, configures D26 RTS,
                            and enables UART8 receive interrupt
remaining IMU/display/vision/encoder initialization
pc_console_init()
```

Verified receive ownership:

```text
LPUART8_IRQHandler
  -> checks UART8 RX-data-ready
  -> pc_console_uart8_rx_isr()
  -> drains real UART8 bytes with uart_query_byte(UART8, ...)
  -> writes them to a dedicated 128-byte SPSC ring buffer

pc_console_poll_input()
  -> consumes the dedicated ISR ring
  -> directly polls UART8 as a fallback
  -> reports every byte
  -> sends non-whitespace ASCII commands to the PC menu
```

Do **not** restore `debug_interrupr_handler()` as the PC command receiver. That
library function writes its cached byte into the debug FIFO even when
`uart_query_byte()` fails, which produced false `hex=00` commands during the
failed test. Do not use the temporary all-UART monitor from build `7100839` in
normal operation either.

### Verified transmit path and success marker

PC-bound text is sent on UART8/D16 with `uart_write_string(DEBUG_UART_INDEX, ...)`
or the equivalent `PC_CONSOLE_UART` wrapper.

Build `7100840` prints:

```text
#WIRELESS_UART8_READY id=7100840 baud=115200 tx=D16 rx=D17
#MCU_BOOT id=7100840 stage=UART8_D16_D17_DEDICATED_RX safe=1
```

When the PC sends ASCII `1`, the verified receive marker is:

```text
UART8_RX id=7100840 tx=D16 rx=D17 src=isr hex=31 ascii='1'
```

`src=poll` is also valid as a fallback, but the real-car successful result was
obtained with the dedicated UART8 configuration above.

### Ownership boundary with the camera

```text
PC/wireless link:  UART8, D16 TX, D17 RX, 115200
OpenART camera:    UART4, C16 TX, C17 RX, 115200
```

UART4/C16/C17 must never be reused by the PC menu, and UART8/D16/D17 must never
be reused by the camera protocol. The links are independent.

### Files and recovery artifacts

```text
project/code/pc_console.c
project/code/pc_console.h
project/user/src/isr.c
project/user/src/main.c

source backup before 7100840:
project/_codex_backups/20260715_011000_uart8_d16_d17_dedicated_rx

verified build log:
project/mdk/codex_uart8_d16_d17_rx_7100840_build.log

verified firmware:
project/mdk/Objects/rt1064.axf
```

Standing rule for future sessions: preserve this UART8 implementation while
adding the menu, solver, mission manager, or controller. If PC commands stop
working, first verify the build ID and the `UART8_RX ... hex=31` marker before
changing protocol logic.

## 47. Queued OpenART fix: force all four map boundaries to walls

Status: recorded only; no visual or MCU source change was made in this turn.
Apply this together with the next requested OpenART-side modification.

Real-car evidence with `map3`:

```text
Expected wall count: 89
Received wall count: 87
Missing cells: (0,10) and (0,11)
Expected M10: #----#.--------#
Received M10: -----#.--------#
Expected M11: ################
Received M11: -###############
```

The UART packet, checksum, MCU parser and MCU map cache were all healthy:
`err=0/0`, `drop=0`, map version advanced, and command `5` reproduced the same
cached map. Therefore this is an OpenART edge-classification issue, not a
transport issue.

Required visual-side rule before committing/printing/transmitting a FULL_MAP:

```text
for every x in [0, width-1]:
    map[0][x] = WALL
    map[height-1][x] = WALL

for every y in [0, height-1]:
    map[y][0] = WALL
    map[y][width-1] = WALL
```

Implementation constraints:

```text
1. Apply the rule after per-cell visual classification but before OA_MAP debug
   printing, wall counting, wall-bit packing and FULL_MAP transmission.
2. Boundary WALL must override floor, goal, box, bomb and temporary car marks.
3. Recompute wall count and wall bitset from the corrected grid; do not only
   change the IDE debug text.
4. Do not alter thresholds, ROI geometry, exposure, VGA resolution, car pose,
   or any interior cell as part of this fix.
5. After the next visual edit, sync the visual script to camera drive G: as
   previously agreed.
```

Acceptance with `map3`:

```text

MCU MAP_BEGIN wall=89
M00 and M11 are all '#'
Every row M00..M11 starts and ends with '#'
box=3 goal=3 bomb=3 remains unchanged
```

## 50. Guarded single-FREE_MOVE follower integrated (2026-07-15)

This stage starts from the real-hardware map2 planner-pass baseline above.
The pre-change backup is:

```text
project/_codex_backups/20260715_025812_map2_planner_pass_before_step_follower
```

Current version markers:

```text
MCU id=7100842 stage=STEP_FREE_MOVE
OpenART oa_id=7100419 (unchanged in this stage)
```

### Scope and safety boundary

Only one complete `ACTION_FREE_MOVE` is enabled per PC command. `PUSH_BOX`,
`PUSH_BOMB`, automatic run-all and all other Action types remain locked. The
unsupported-action check occurs before `device_init_flag=0`, so a push Action
cannot accidentally enable motion. The verified speed command is 200 in the
current speed-loop unit (encoder count per 5 ms), not PWM.

The new execution path is:

```text
cached solver_output_t
  -> mission_manager arm/interlock
  -> action_follower one FREE_MOVE Action
  -> visual x10 grid pose and grid-center waypoints
  -> map direction -> body direction
  -> existing motion heading lock + kinematics + wheel PID
```

New files:

```text
project/code/action_follower.c/.h
project/code/mission_manager.c/.h
```

Both C and header files are included in `project/mdk/rt1064.uvprojx`.

### Coordinate and motion conventions

The visual pose stores `(x,y)` in 0.1-cell units. A cell center `(m,n)` is
still `(m*200,n*200)` mm, so the follower converts a visual coordinate with
`x_mm=x10*20`, `y_mm=y10*20` and does not add 100 mm.

Map compass angles are `0=up, 90=right, 180=down, 270=left`. The control-layer
test established that body command 90 degrees is physical forward. At Action
start the follower captures the visual vehicle heading and uses:

```text
body_command = normalize(90 + map_direction - visual_heading)
```

The existing IMU heading target is captured once and held while moving,
paused and stopped. Follower updates do not call the old
`motion_set_velocity()` repeatedly, because that API resets position and yaw
targets on every call. The added heading-lock update API changes translation
direction/speed without resetting those closed-loop states.

### Mission state machine and interlocks

States:

```text
SAFE_IDLE -> STEP_WAIT -> ACTION_RUNNING -> STEP_WAIT/COMPLETE
                         -> PAUSED -> ACTION_RUNNING
any active stage -> FAULT or hard-stop/disarm
```

Interlocks:

```text
- MCU boots hard-stopped; entering menu 3 does not enable motors.
- E arms only a valid/current cached plan and still leaves motors disabled.
- E also checks that the car is still within 0.6 cell of the pose used to solve.
- N enables motion only after a FREE_MOVE follower starts successfully.
- stale/offline pose, no progress for 5 s, Action timeout, map-content change,
  or follower fault stops and disarms the mission.
- periodic identical FULL_MAP packets may increment map_version without
  invalidating the plan; semantic map content is protected by a map hash.
- after any Action has advanced the cursor, resetting/rearming the old route is
  rejected and the operator must disarm and solve again at the current pose.
- X/K/B use hard stop; automatic run-all remains locked.
```

The Action follower uses a 20 ms update period, 300 ms pose-stale threshold,
70 mm waypoint arrival radius, 20 mm progress threshold, 5 s stall timeout and
180 s maximum Action time.

### UART8 run menu

Main-menu item 3 now opens:

```text
E  arm current cached plan; no movement
N  execute the next FREE_MOVE Action
P  pause with stationary heading hold
C  continue a paused Action
R  reset cursor only before the first Action
K  disarm and hard stop
A  run all (locked)
S  print mission/follower status
B  disarm and return to main menu
X  emergency stop
H  print menu
```

During movement, UART8 prints one `MISSION`/`FOLLOW` report per second. State
changes print `MISSION_EVENT` immediately. Important expected results:

```text
E -> RUN_CMD ARM result=OK, state=STEP_WAIT, motor remains LOCKED
N -> RUN_CMD NEXT result=OK, state=ACTION_RUNNING, motor ENABLED
first map2 FREE Action complete -> state=STEP_WAIT cursor=1/10
next N on map2 -> UNSUPPORTED_ACTION; cursor remains 1 and no push starts
P/C -> PAUSED/ACTION_RUNNING
K/B/X -> SAFE_IDLE, armed=0, motor LOCKED
```

## 51. 250 ms run telemetry and UART8 menu toggle (2026-07-15)

The user reported that menu `3 -> N` moves the car but the trajectory is
clearly wrong. This stage adds read-only diagnostic output before changing any
coordinate transform, control gain or motor command.

Pre-change rollback backup:

```text
project/_codex_backups/20260715_043616_step_free_move_before_telemetry
```

Current version markers:

```text
MCU id=7100843 stage=STEP_FREE_MOVE_TELEMETRY
OpenART oa_id=7100419 (unchanged)
```

### Telemetry switch and timing

Run-menu command `T` toggles periodic telemetry. It defaults to ON at boot and
the menu always displays the current ON/OFF state. The interval is 50 PIT
ticks, therefore 250 ms at the verified 5 ms control period. Disabling `T`
stops periodic `RUN_TELEM`/`RUN_CTRL` and waypoint messages, but safety/state
events remain enabled.

Periodic output while `ACTION_RUNNING` or `PAUSED`:

```text
RUN_TELEM id=7100843 seq=... ms=... state=... motor=... plan=...
  action=... wp=... pose=LIVE car=(x,y) age=... target=(x_mm,y_mm)
  delta=(dx_mm,dy_mm) dist=... vtheta=... ref=...
  elapsed=... progress=...

RUN_CTRL seq=... map_dir=... body_cmd=... applied=(angle,speed)
  yaw=(now=... target=... err=... corr=...)
  wheel_t[R,LF,RF]=(...) enc=(...) pwm=(...)
```

`R`, `LF`, `RF` are explicitly rear, left-front and right-front. The snapshot
contains the wheel targets after kinematics, signed encoder speeds and final
PWM values. Motion variables updated by the 5 ms ISR are copied inside a very
short interrupt-protected critical section; telemetry never writes them.

Immediate messages:

```text
RUN_EVENT START action=... wp=.../...
RUN_EVENT WAYPOINT action=... old=... new=... total=...
MISSION_EVENT plus a final telemetry pair on arrival or fault
```

Follower diagnostics now retain visual pose age, start visual heading, target
delta, distance, elapsed time and time since meaningful progress. A latched
follower fault is no longer erased before UART reporting; `STALLED`,
`POSE_STALE`, `VISION_OFFLINE`, `TIMEOUT` and `BAD_ACTION` therefore remain
visible after the mandatory hard stop. Sending `K`, `B` or `X` later clears the
latched follower diagnostics normally.

Before `N`, follower pose has intentionally not been captured. The old
misleading zero line is replaced with:

```text
FOLLOW state=IDLE fault=NONE pose=NOT_CAPTURED ...
```

The live camera pose and theta remain available in `STATUS`; the follower
captures them only when a FREE_MOVE actually starts.

## 52. Body-direction transform sign fixed from real telemetry (2026-07-15, superseded by section 54)

Real run telemetry from MCU 7100843 isolated the first physical follower bug.
The tested map started at visual pose `(1.9,2.9)` with visual heading about
103 degrees. Action 0 correctly skipped waypoint 0 near `(2,3)` and selected
waypoint 1 at `(2,4)`.

Observed planning values were internally correct:

```text
target=(400,800) mm
delta=(20,220) mm
map_dir=174.8 deg (almost straight down on the screen)
```

The old conversion produced:

```text
body_cmd=161.8 deg
wheel_t[R,LF,RF]=(-81,-152,176)
```

The wheel encoders eventually followed the requested signs and PWM stayed
well below the +/-10000 limits, so neither encoder correspondence, wheel PID
sign nor PWM saturation was the primary failure. The visual position failed
to approach the target because the map-to-body transform mirrored the lateral
component.

Coordinate definitions confirmed by code and previous physical tests:

```text
map heading: clockwise from screen-up
  0=up, 90=right, 180=down, 270=left
body angle: counter-clockwise from body-right
  0=right, 90=forward, 180=left, 270=backward
```

Therefore the correct conversion is:

```text
body_command = normalize(90 + current_visual_heading - map_direction)
```

This supersedes the incorrect formula recorded in section 50. The follower
now uses the current live visual theta on every update, while the start theta
remains in telemetry as `ref` for drift comparison.

For the captured frames the corrected results are:

```text
map=174.8, vtheta=107.1 -> body=22.3 deg
map=175.2, vtheta=103.0 -> body=17.8 deg
map=169.7, vtheta=98.7  -> body=19.0 deg
```


about 2.9 toward 4.0.

## 53. Visual marker heading is 180 degrees opposite the physical front (2026-07-15, superseded by section 54)

The first physical run of MCU 7100844 proved that the algebraic sign fix in
section 52 was necessary but not sufficient. Planning and waypoint selection
were correct:

```text
car=(9.1,6.6)
target=(1600,1400) mm
delta=(-220,+80) mm
map_dir=250.0 deg
```

The vehicle should therefore have moved left and slightly down in the camera
map. Instead it moved to `(9.5,5.9)`, almost exactly right and up. Encoder
signs broadly followed their wheel targets and PWM stayed below saturation,
so wheel correspondence, encoder polarity and PID gains were not changed.

The remaining mismatch is the semantic direction of the two-color vehicle
marker: the raw OpenART vector reported as `vtheta` points 180 degrees opposite
the physical vehicle front used by the verified body-motion controller. The
MCU follower now contains an explicit, isolated adapter:

```text
physical_heading = normalize(vtheta + 180)
body_command = normalize(90 + physical_heading - map_direction)
```

For the representative bad frame this changes:

```text
vtheta=85.2, map_dir=250.0
old body_cmd=285.2
used physical heading=265.2
new body_cmd=105.2
```

Telemetry now prints both the raw visual angle and the angle used by control:

```text
vtheta=<raw OpenART theta> ref=<raw theta at Action start> used=<vtheta+180>
```

This is deliberately implemented only in `project/code/action_follower.c`.
The OpenART protocol and recognition code are unchanged, making the correction
easy to verify and reverse without disturbing map recognition or UART framing.

## 54. Map-to-body transform calibrated from two physical runs (2026-07-15)

MCU 7100845 correctly applied the temporary 180-degree offset, but its real
run still moved away from the waypoint. The action target was left and slightly
up from the vehicle:

```text
car=(9.9,7.3)
target=(1800,1400) mm
delta=(-180,-60) mm
map_dir=288.4 deg
vtheta=94.4 deg
temporary body_cmd=76.0 deg
```

The vehicle then moved to `(10.1,9.4)` and distance grew from `190` to `528 mm`.
This is not a planner error. The 7100845 IMU yaw stayed within about +/-4
degrees and wheel encoders followed their target signs, so yaw instability,
wheel mapping and PID tuning were not the cause.

Combining both physical runs gives a direct calibration identity:

```text
MCU 7100844: vtheta 85.2 + body_cmd 285.2 = map motion about 10 deg
              observed motion was screen-up/right, about 30 deg

MCU 7100845: vtheta 94.4 + body_cmd 76.0 = map motion about 170 deg
              observed motion was almost screen-down, about 175 deg
```

Therefore:

```text
actual_map_motion = normalize(vtheta + body_command)
body_command = normalize(map_direction - vtheta)
```

This also agrees with the earlier physical mode-5 test: body command `0 deg`
is physical forward and `90 deg` is physical right. The raw OpenART `vtheta`
already represents the physical vehicle front in map coordinates; it must not
be negated or offset by 90/180 degrees.

Authoritative coordinate definitions from this point forward:

```text
map_direction and vtheta: clockwise from screen-up
  0=up, 90=right, 180=down, 270=left

body_command: clockwise from physical vehicle-forward
  0=forward, 90=right, 180=backward, 270=left

conversion:
  body_command = normalize(map_direction - vtheta)
```

`used` remains in telemetry as the calibrated heading used by the follower.
Its configurable offset is now zero, so `used` must equal `vtheta`.

For the latest captured frame, MCU 7100846 must produce:

```text
vtheta=94.4 used=94.4 map_dir=288.4 body_cmd=194.0
```

```text
project/_codex_backups/20260715_051847_heading_offset_7100845_before_measured_frame_fix
```

## 55. Visual correction added to the IMU heading integrator (2026-07-15)

The physical run of MCU 7100846 confirmed the map/body transform from section
54. Every telemetry frame satisfied approximately:

```text
normalize(vtheta + body_cmd) = map_dir
```

For example, `90.0 + 164.1 = 254.1 deg`. The transform must not be changed
again without contradictory physical evidence.

The Action nevertheless failed to approach waypoint `(10,7)`. Position stayed
around `(10.7,6.8)`, and distance oscillated between `146` and `165 mm`. The
new primary fault was a split between visual and inertial heading:

```text
visual vtheta: approximately 85.6 to 94.4 deg around a 90.0-deg reference
IMU relative yaw: 3.5 -> 6.2 -> 13.6 -> 35.0 -> 50.4 -> 89.1 deg
yaw correction: reached -50 limit and remained saturated
```

The system planning document explicitly requires IMU as the high-rate heading
source and visual theta as calibration/anomaly input. The previous code only
implemented IMU integration and never applied visual calibration.

MCU 7100847 adds bounded complementary correction while a follower Action is
active:

```text
visual_relative = shortest_delta(current_vtheta, Action_start_vtheta)
expected_imu_yaw = held_imu_target + visual_relative
sync_error = shortest_delta(expected_imu_yaw, integrated_imu_yaw)

deadband = 2.0 deg
correction gain = 0.35 per new visual pose frame
maximum correction step = 4.0 deg per visual pose frame
```

The 5-ms gyro integration and original yaw PID remain active. Visual input does
not replace the IMU and cannot snap the heading by an unbounded amount; it only
pulls accumulated drift back toward the absolute visual attitude. Correction
is applied only when `pose.last_packet_tick` changes.

Run telemetry now includes:

```text
gyro=<current gyro Z rate in deg/s>
vsync=<visual/IMU discrepancy>/<correction applied this visual frame>
pid_t=(rear,left-front,right-front effective speed-PID targets)
enc_rot=<mean of the three wheel encoders, rotation indicator>
```

This also exposes a secondary issue from the 7100846 run. The right-front
target was often `+139..+170`, but its encoder was mostly `0..35` despite PWM
around `+2800..+4000`, while the other wheels responded more strongly. Do not
change its known physical mapping or PID yet. Use 7100847 `pid_t/enc/pwm` to
separate position-loop compensation, closed-loop response and mechanical load.


```text
0 Error(s), 0 Warning(s)
Code=105632 RO-data=12884 RW-data=596 ZI-data=161864
firmware: project/mdk/Objects/rt1064.axf
```

encoder signs, direction levels, installation angles and all existing PID
gains/limits. OpenART source and protocol are unchanged.

## 57. MCU 7100848 run isolates right-front wheel response failure (2026-07-15)

The speed-100 physical run made substantial early progress through the first
FREE_MOVE: visual x moved `10.3 -> 9.9 -> 9.3 -> 9.1 -> 7.6`, and waypoint
indices advanced from 1 to 3. This confirms the planner, map/body transform,
speed change and initial visual/IMU correction path are active.

The vehicle then rotated and left the path. Both visual theta and gyro changed
strongly, so the later yaw motion was real rather than only inertial drift. The
decisive channel evidence is the right-front wheel telemetry:

```text
RF wheel_target: commonly +80 .. +160
RF speed-PID target: commonly +100 .. +166
RF PWM: commonly +2000 .. +3365
RF encoder: almost always 0, occasionally 1
```

At the same time the left-front and rear encoders responded whenever their
targets became large enough. The asymmetric wheel response caused chassis
rotation, rapidly changing visual theta and eventually producing a legitimate
`STALLED` follower fault. `NEXT result=NOT_ARMED` after that fault is expected,
because the fault path disarms the mission.

Static software audit reconfirmed the authoritative mapping:

```text
right-front motor: PWM C6, DIR C7, forward_level 1
right-front encoder: QTIMER1_ENCODER1 on C0/C1, encoder_sign -1
```

This matches the previously verified mapping. No other current initialization
reconfigures C0/C1. The generic GPIO ISR contains a legacy C0 flag check but
there is no `exti_init(C0)`, so it does not explain a continuously zero QTIMER
count and should not be edited speculatively.

Do not change wheel mapping, encoder sign, PID gains, body transform or yaw
fusion until the physical observation is known: determine whether the
right-front wheel itself rotates during the test. If it does not rotate, inspect
motor power/wiring/driver/dead-zone. If it rotates but C0/C1 remains zero, inspect
the encoder cable, power and signal pair or rerun the dedicated open-loop
motor/encoder diagnostic before changing software.

No source or firmware was changed after analyzing this 7100848 run.

## 58. 重点候选基线：7100848，等待右前轮硬件修复确认 (2026-07-15)

### 58.1 当前版本定位

将当前版本明确标记为：

```text
FREE_MOVE 软件链路候选成功版
状态：软件通路已基本验证，受右前轮硬件/反馈通道阻塞
不是最终比赛完整版本：PUSH_BOX 尚未完成实车验证
```

当前识别信息：

```text
MCU id=7100848 stage=FOLLOW_SPEED_100
OpenART oa_id=7100419
FREE_MOVE speed=100 encoder counts / 5 ms
PC无线调试串口：UART8，TX D16，RX D17，115200
MCU视觉串口：UART4，TX C16，RX C17，115200
```

### 58.2 为什么这一版应重点保留

7100848 的实车日志已经证明以下软件数据流实际工作，而不仅是静态
编译通过：

```text
OpenART位置帧/全图帧
-> vision_link收帧、校验和地图缓存
-> PC菜单主动请求地图
-> solver根据地图和车辆格子生成Action/waypoint
-> mission_manager安全解锁和分步执行
-> action_follower消费实时视觉位姿
-> 地图方向转车体方向
-> IMU高频航向 + 视觉角度受限校准
-> 三轮运动学、速度/位置/航向闭环
-> PWM输出与扩展遥测
```

已观察到车辆位置：

```text
x: 10.3 -> 9.9 -> 9.3 -> 9.1 -> 7.6
waypoint: 1 -> 2 -> 3
```

因此地图解析、规划、菜单、任务状态机、正确方向换算和前半段
FREE_MOVE 都已真实运行。后来偏航并触发 `STALLED`，证据指向单独的
右前轮通道，并不能推翻上述软件链路。

### 58.3 当前权威控制定义，禁止在硬件确认前改乱

地图/车体方向定义：

```text
map_direction与vtheta：从屏幕正上方开始顺时针
body_command：从物理车头开始顺时针
body_command = normalize(map_direction - vtheta)
```

轮子映射：

```text
rear:
  PWM C10, DIR C11, forward_level 1
  encoder C2/C24, QTIMER1_ENCODER2, encoder_sign -1
  speed PID Kp=18, Ki=0.03, Kd=0

left-front:
  PWM C8, DIR C9, forward_level 0
  encoder C5/C25, QTIMER2_ENCODER2, encoder_sign -1
  speed PID Kp=18, Ki=0.03, Kd=0

right-front:
  PWM C6, DIR C7, forward_level 1
  encoder C0/C1, QTIMER1_ENCODER1, encoder_sign -1
  speed PID Kp=20, Ki=0.03, Kd=0
```

现有位置环和航向环参数同样保留。视觉/IMU校准只在新视觉姿态帧
到达时工作，死区2度、增益0.35、单帧最多修正4度。不要因为本次
偏航重新引入90/180度偏置，也不要交换电机、编码器或PID归属。

### 58.4 唯一已定位阻塞点

7100848 的右前轮遥测：

```text
wheel_target: 常见 +80 .. +160
speed-PID target: 常见 +100 .. +166
PWM: 常见 +2000 .. +3365
encoder C0/C1: 几乎一直为0，偶尔为1
```

另外两轮在目标足够大时都有编码器反馈。右前轮不响应使车辆由其余
轮子主导而旋转，随后视觉theta和陀螺仪都检测到真实转动，最后
`STALLED`保护停车。故障后 `N -> NOT_ARMED` 是正常安全行为。

硬件检查分支：

```text
右前轮本身不转：检查电机线、驱动输出、供电、方向线和启动死区。
右前轮会转但C0/C1仍为0：检查编码器供电、插头、C0/C1信号线和编码器本体。
```

### 58.5 重点备份

```text
backup/candidate_7100848_hardware_pending_20260715_054132
```

备份内容包括：

```text
project/code完整控制与中间层源码
project/user/src启动和中断源码
Keil工程、AXF和关键构建日志
OpenART新视觉main.py
更新记忆前后的接力记忆
BACKUP_INFO.md
```

制品校验：

```text
AXF SHA256:
C3D60139BC3852B77743987842561FC8D9852AA0C0E9897BBB2C768679CC008D

OpenART SHA256:
7D8D6E67A6D65CD5DA623793986BAEFB90A32D7BB33DF808905FF7AACCB62C15

build log:
project/mdk/codex_follow_speed100_7100848_full_build.log
0 Error(s), 0 Warning(s)
Code=105632 RO-data=12876 RW-data=596 ZI-data=161864
```

### 58.6 修好硬件后的恢复与复测顺序

1. 保持当前软件映射，先确认右前轮电机能转且C0/C1有非零计数。
2. 下载候选备份中的 `project/mdk/Objects/rt1064.axf`。
3. 串口必须看到 `MCU_BOOT id=7100848 stage=FOLLOW_SPEED_100`。
4. 依次执行 `1 -> 2 -> D -> B -> 3 -> E -> N`，只测试第一条FREE_MOVE。
5. 保留至少十组 `RUN_TELEM/RUN_CTRL`，重点检查三轮 `pid_t/enc/pwm`。
6. 正常标准：右前轮编码器随目标同号且明显非零、`dist`总体下降、
   waypoint按序推进、航向不会持续发散、最终Action结束或安全到点。
7. FREE_MOVE确认后再进入PUSH_BOX实车实现与验证，不要直接解锁Run All。

恢复原则：如果后续调试把软件改乱，优先整体恢复此候选备份，不要只
零散复制一个 `motion_control.c`，以免菜单、mission、follower和遥测
版本不匹配。

## 2026-07-16 MCU Mode1 Encoder Mapping Diagnostic

Purpose: replace the previous mode-1 100 ms instantaneous encoder print with a cleaner 2 s accumulated motor/encoder mapping test over DEBUG UART8.

MCU id: 7100857
Updated side: MCU only. OpenART/vision unchanged.
Backup before edit:
`backup\mcu_before_mode1_accum_maptest_20260716_021116`

Serial output remains:
`DEBUG_UART_INDEX = UART8`, baud `115200`, TX `D16`, RX `D17`.

Current mode:
`CONTROL_VERIFY_MODE = 1`

New mode-1 behavior cycles automatically:

```text
OFF0 -> REAR -> OFF1 -> LEFT-FRONT -> OFF2 -> RIGHT-FRONT -> repeat
```

Each stage:
- Clears encoder counts continuously in 100 ms chunks.
- Accumulates 20 chunks = 2000 ms.
- Prints one UART line:

```text
MAPTEST id=7100857 mode=1 stage=... stage_name=... active=... active_name=... pwm=... sample_ms=2000 rear_sum=... left_sum=... right_sum=... last=...
```

Interpretation:
- OFF stages should be near zero if the car is lifted and wheels are not touched.
- If OFF stages have large counts, suspect encoder noise, floating lines, wiring interference, or wheel movement unrelated to the commanded motor.
- In each motor stage, the encoder with the dominant accumulated count should identify the encoder physically paired with that motor command.
- Keep the car lifted during this test; if it sits on the ground, one powered wheel can move the chassis and drag other wheels, making all encoders nonzero.

Current hardware table in `project/code/motion_control.c` at this point:

```text
wheel0 rear        encoder QTIMER1_ENCODER2 C2/C24 sign -1 PWM C10 DIR C11 forward_level 1
wheel1 left-front  encoder QTIMER2_ENCODER2 C5/C25 sign -1 PWM C8  DIR C9  forward_level 1
wheel2 right-front encoder QTIMER1_ENCODER1 C0/C1  sign +1 PWM C6  DIR C7  forward_level 1
```

## 2026-07-16 MCU Encoder-Only Software Exclusion Test

Reason: mode-1 auto map test showed very large encoder counts even during OFF stages:
left encoder often 60k-99k over 2 seconds while PWM was reported as 0.

MCU id: 7100858
Updated side: MCU only. OpenART/vision unchanged.
Backup before edit:
`backup\mcu_before_encoder_only_diag_20260716_021957`

Current diagnostic switch:
`ENCODER_ONLY_DIAG = 1`

Behavior:
- After `clock_init()` and `debug_init()`, MCU immediately enters encoder-only loop.
- It does not call `motor_io_init()`.
- It does not initialize motor PWM or direction GPIO.
- It does not start PIT, IMU, vision, odometry, screen, or closed-loop control.
- It initializes only the three quadrature encoders and prints one 2-second accumulated sample through DEBUG UART8.

Expected UART:

```text
#ENCODER_ONLY_DIAG_START id=7100858 no_motor_pwm no_pit no_control
ENCODERONLY id=7100858 sample_ms=2000 rear_sum=... left_sum=... right_sum=... last=...
```

Interpretation:
- With the car still and wheels not touched, all three sums should be near zero.
- If large counts still appear, especially on the left encoder C5/C25, the cause is almost certainly hardware/electrical: encoder wiring, floating input, wrong power/ground, interference, damaged encoder, or channel short/crosstalk.
- If this test is near zero but the previous MAPTEST OFF stages are large, then motor PWM initialization or motor-driver wiring/noise is involved.

User observation:

```text

Previous mode 1 observation: left-front open-loop positive test used C9=0, wheel rotated clockwise, raw encoder approx -8700.
```

Software correction:

```text
MCU id: 7100861
project/code/motion_control.c

  encoder QTIMER2_ENCODER2 C5/C25
  encoder_sign: +1 -> -1
  forward_level: 1 -> 0

```

Reasoning:
- The left-front closed-loop positive direction is now aligned with the observed mode-1 open-loop positive direction: `C9=0`.
- Raw negative encoder counts from that direction are mapped to positive signed speed by `encoder_sign=-1`.
- This should make mode 3 position-hold correction oppose hand movement instead of amplifying it.

`main.c` was also switched to direct mode-3 testing:

```text

FEEDBACK_TEST_NO_UART = 1
```

## 2026-07-16 Wheel Rotation Direction Convention

User-defined convention for all future motor rotation descriptions:

```text
All clockwise / counterclockwise descriptions refer to the real motor-wheel rotation direction
as viewed from the car center looking outward toward that motor/wheel.
```

This is intentionally not the opposite outside-looking-in view.

Practical meaning:

```text
If the motor/wheel is described as clockwise, that same wheel action tends to rotate the whole car clockwise.
If the motor/wheel is described as counterclockwise, that same wheel action tends to rotate the whole car counterclockwise.
```

Use this convention when interpreting mode-1 open-loop rotation, mode-3 hand-turn feedback tests, encoder sign, and `forward_level`.

```text
CONTROL_VERIFY_MOTOR = 1

physical rotation: clockwise
raw encoder count: about -8700
```

Important interpretation:
- Mode 1 is open-loop single-motor test and is suitable for collecting motor direction and raw encoder sign.
- Mode 2 is open-loop all-motor positive PWM test, so it does not and should not provide negative feedback.
- Negative-feedback validation should be done with mode 3/4/5, especially mode 3 for wheel position hold.

## 2026-07-16 MCU Test Build 7100862: Left-Front Direction Pin Toggle

Purpose:
- Diagnose whether the left-front motor direction pin `C9` actually reverses the physical motor direction.
- This is a hardware-direction test, not a PID/closed-loop test.

Current MCU change:

```text
project/user/src/main.c
CONTROL_VERIFY_MODE = 6
CONTROL_VERIFY_PWM = 2000
firmware/display id = 7100862
```

Mode 6 behavior:

```text
Only left-front motor is driven:
  left-front PWM = C8
  left-front DIR = C9
  PWM duty = CONTROL_VERIFY_PWM

Rear and right-front PWM are forced to 0.
C9 toggles every about 3 seconds:
  C9=0 for about 3s
  C9=1 for about 3s
  repeat
```

Screen output:

```text
M6 LF C9 DIR TOGGLE
ID:7100862
C9 LEVEL: 0 or 1
left/rear/right raw encoder increments
```

User should report, using the established center-looking-outward direction convention:

```text
C9=0: physical direction = clockwise/counterclockwise, left raw = +/- value
C9=1: physical direction = clockwise/counterclockwise, left raw = +/- value
whether only the left-front wheel turns
```

Interpretation:
- If `C9=0` and `C9=1` produce opposite physical directions, the direction pin works and software can map `forward_level` correctly.
- If both levels produce the same physical direction, the issue is likely wiring/driver-side direction control on the left-front motor path, not PID signs.

Confirmed result after replacing the faulty motor driver:

```text
The previous driver was bad and made C9=0 / C9=1 rotate in the same direction.
After changing the driver, mode 3 position-hold feedback is normal.

Left-front direction pin result:
  C9 = 0 -> clockwise
  C9 = 1 -> counterclockwise
```

Use the established direction convention: clockwise/counterclockwise is viewed from the car center outward toward the motor.

Backup before mode-6 edit:

```text
backup/mcu_before_mode6_lf_dir_toggle_20260716_072625
```

```text
project/mdk/build_mode6_lf_dir_toggle_7100862.log
```

## 2026-07-16 MCU Build 7100863: Right-Front Positive Direction Flip

User observation:

```text
In mode 4 yaw-hold test, after rotating the chassis by hand, the right-front wheel turned opposite to the rear and left-front wheels.
That is wrong because mode 4 rotation correction should command all three wheel targets with the same sign.
```

Code reasoning:

```text
k_calculate() adds rotate_speed with the same sign to all three wheels.
Therefore mode 4 yaw correction expects the three wheel positive directions to represent the same physical chassis rotation direction.
```

Previous right-front mapping:

```text
wheel2 right-front:
  encoder_sign = +1
  PWM = C6
  DIR = C7
  forward_level = 1

Known mode-1 observation:
  C7 = 1 made the right-front wheel rotate counterclockwise.
```

Fix applied:

```text
project/code/motion_control.c
wheel2 right-front:
  encoder_sign: +1 -> -1
  forward_level: 1 -> 0
  PWM C6 and DIR C7 unchanged
```

Reason for changing both values:
- Flipping only `forward_level` would reverse the motor's physical positive direction, but would also make the speed loop read the opposite encoder sign and risk positive feedback.
- Flipping both `forward_level` and `encoder_sign` keeps closed-loop feedback negative while making the right-front positive wheel direction match the rear and left-front in mode 4.

Current test firmware:

```text
project/user/src/main.c
CONTROL_VERIFY_MODE = 4
display/log id = 7100863
```

Backup before edit:

```text
backup/mcu_before_rf_positive_direction_flip_20260716_082427
```

Build result:

```text
project/mdk/build_rf_positive_direction_7100863.log
0 Error(s), 0 Warning(s)
```

```c
#define CONTROL_VERIFY_MOTOR   (2U)

```

- `CONTROL_DEBUG_ENABLE=0`：正式模式。保留 UART8 菜单、UART4 视觉通信、地图、规划器、任务管理器、Action 跟随器及全部安全门；上电仍停车等待 PC 指令。
- `CONTROL_DEBUG_ENABLE=1`：隔离调试模式。不初始化视觉、规划、任务管理和 PC 菜单；由 `CONTROL_VERIFY_MODE` 选择 1~6，默认 6。
- 修改开关或模式后必须重新编译并下载，不能在运行中切换。

六个调试模式：

```text
1  单电机开环；CONTROL_VERIFY_MOTOR 选择 0后轮/1左前/2右前
2  三电机按当前物理正方向同时开环
3  三轮位置保持，手拧后应负反馈拉回；专用 PWM 限幅 1800
4  IMU 航向保持，手转车体后车轮应反向修正
5  速度 300 向右平移并保持航向；限幅由 MODE5_LIMIT 开关控制
6  只驱动左前轮，每 3 秒切换 C9=0/1，并显示三个原始编码器读数
```

开环调试采用当前已确认的物理正方向电平：`rear C11=1`、`LF C9=0`、
`RF C7=0`。模式 6 屏幕明确显示 `C9=0 CW`、`C9=1 CCW`；旋转方向仍按
“从车中心朝电机方向看”的统一约定。

## 2026-07-17：UART8 非阻塞遥测发送（当前最新）

当前 MCU 识别号为 `7100869`，启动阶段标记为 `UART8_NONBLOCKING_TX`。视觉端本次未修改。
正式模式配置保持为：

```c
#define CONTROL_DEBUG_ENABLE   (0U)
#define CONTROL_VERIFY_MODE    (3U)
#define RACE_FREE_MOVE_SPEED   (100.0f)
```

修改原因：`7100868` 的一组完整 PID 遥测约 1370 字节，UART8 以 115200 波特率阻塞发送时，
每 250ms 会连续占用主循环约 119ms。5ms 电机 PID 仍由 PIT 运行，但主循环中的
`vision_link_poll -> mission_manager_poll/action_follower_poll` 会被延迟，速度 150 时可能因此
带着旧方向越过 70mm 航点捕获半径。

当前 `project/code/pc_console.c` 已把所有 PC 菜单和运行遥测改为 8KB 环形发送队列：

```text
UART8: 115200, TX=D16, RX=D17
TX mode: NONBLOCKING_QUEUE
TX queue: 8192 bytes
每次 pc_console_poll 最多尝试写 16 字节；只在 TDRE 可写时写入，不等待硬件
原 250ms 详细遥测的字段、周期和内容均未改变
UART8 RX 中断与菜单命令接收逻辑未改变
```

`4` 状态命令新增字段：

```text
txq     当前待发送字节数
txpeak  上电以来队列最高占用
txdrop  因队列空间不足而整行丢弃的次数；正常必须为 0
```

这次为单变量验证，以下内容均未修改：航点到达阈值仍为 70mm、近点最低速度仍为100、
500ms 航点停车与位置重置时机不变、全部速度/位置/航向 PID 参数不变、视觉端不变。
下一次应在相同地图和近似起点分别跑速度100、150的 `3N`，比较 `7100868` 与 `7100869`；
测试结束发送 `4`，确认 `txdrop=0` 并记录 `txpeak`。

## 2026-07-17：航点停车位置重基准与出口复核（当前最新）

当前 MCU 识别号更新为 `7100870`，启动阶段标记为
`WAYPOINT_HOLD_RECHECK`。视觉端本次未修改，仍使用原有 OpenART
程序与协议。正式模式保持 `CONTROL_DEBUG_ENABLE=0`，用户当前设置的
`RACE_FREE_MOVE_SPEED=150.0f`、所有速度/位置/航向 PID、PWM 限幅、
70 mm 到达阈值和 500 ms 航点停车时间均未改动。

`7100869` 的速度 150 日志确认：车辆进入 `WP_HOLD` 后，目标速度虽然为
0，但旧路段累计的位置误差仍被静止位置环继续补偿，车辆从距离航点
82 mm 漂到 216 mm，航向也从约 85.6 度偏到 59.7 度；旧状态机在
500 ms 到期后仍无条件切换下一航点。非阻塞 UART8 已排除主循环长时间
等待，但不是该运动异常的主因。

本版在 `project/code/action_follower.c` 做了两项定点修正：

```text
1. 进入中间航点 WP_HOLD：
   motion_heading_lock_stop()
   -> motion_heading_lock_rebase_position()
   -> 清空三轮旧位置误差、位置保持状态和速度 PID 状态
   -> 从停车入口的真实位置开始统计停车期间的新位移

2. 500 ms 停车结束：
   -> 再次重建三轮位置基准
   -> 用最新视觉位置重新计算到当前航点的距离
   -> 距离 <= 100 mm：允许切换下一航点
   -> 距离 > 100 mm：不增加 waypoint_index，重新靠近当前航点
```

`100 mm` 是停车出口容许半径，和原 `70 mm` 进入半径构成 30 mm
迟滞，避免视觉 20 mm 量化和停车小幅位移造成反复切换。每个正常完成的
停车阶段会在入口和出口各重基准一次，所以遥测里的 `rebase` 通常每个
航点增加 2。若看到 `WP_RESUME` 但前面没有 `RUN_EVENT WAYPOINT old/new`，
表示出口复核未通过，跟随器正在重新追当前航点；这属于预期保护行为。

静态数据流已复核：

```text
UART4 C16/C17 -> vision_link_poll
-> mission_manager_poll
-> action_follower_poll
-> motion_heading_lock_stop/rebase/update
-> PIT 每 5 ms 调用 motor_control_5ms
-> 三轮位置环、速度环、航向环和 PWM/DIR
```

编译和固件检查：

### 目的与物理初值

本模式暂时脱离地图规划，使用“当前位置为局部原点”的短距离实验，把电机闭环、
三轮编码器、IMU 和视觉位移分开验证。当前物理初值为：

```text
场地：3200 mm x 2400 mm，16 x 12 格
单格：200 mm
轮胎直径：58 mm（motion_control.c 已由旧 65 mm 改为 58 mm）
编码器：2048 count/rev，电机与编码器暂按 1:1
单脉冲距离：约 0.08899 mm/count
轮心到车中心：100 mm
平移测试速度选项：100 / 120 / 150 count/5ms
控制周期：5 ms；点测试状态更新：20 ms；到点停车：500 ms
视觉坐标量化：一格 10.0，对应 200 mm，因此视觉 0.1 格对应 20 mm
```

`motion_control.c` 新增三轮有符号累计脉冲，并开放局部测试需要的接口：

```text
motion_get_wheel_total_counts
motion_get_mm_per_count
motion_set_yaw_hold_enable
motion_set_manual_rotation / motion_stop_manual_rotation
```

静止航向保持输出现在也使用原有 `YAW_HOLD_OUT_LIMIT=50` 限幅；所有轮速、
位置环和航向 PID 参数没有改动。纯旋转仍走现有三轮闭环，只增加了可由菜单
指定正负旋转速度的上层入口。

### 新增状态机与安全规则

新增 `project/code/point_test.c/.h`，状态为：

```text
LOCKED -> Z捕获原点 -> READY -> R启动 -> RUNNING
-> 到目标后 SETTLING 500ms -> DONE并锁电机
异常 -> FAULT并锁电机
X -> LOCKED、锁电机并使原点失效
```

每一次运动前都必须重新发送 `Z`。只允许在 `READY` 启动；运行中不能改方向、
格数、速度或传感器模式。平移具有超时、编码器超程 300 mm、横向偏移 400 mm、
M3 视觉超时 300 ms保护；旋转超时 20 s。故障和正常结束都会把
`device_init_flag` 置1并硬停车。

平移传感器模式：

```text
M1 ENCODER+IMU：编码器决定到点，IMU保持Z时航向；首选基准测试
M2 ENCODER_OPEN_YAW：编码器决定到点，关闭IMU航向纠偏；用于看机械自然偏航
M3 VISION+IMU：视觉决定到点，IMU保持航向；必须在Z和运行期间保持视觉在线
M4 FUSION：特意锁定，当前尚未实现，设置接口会拒绝
```

旋转可在 IMU 和 ENCODER 两种停止源之间切换。编码器旋转角暂按：

```text
三轮平均累计脉冲 * mm/count / 100mm * 180/pi
```

它是待实测校准量，不能在第一轮就当作真值；IMU短时角度作为高置信参考。

### UART8 菜单

PC 无线调试串口固定为 `UART8 115200, TX=D16, RX=D17`，仍使用非阻塞8KB TX队列。
主菜单：

```text
1 平移测试
2 原地旋转测试
3 锁车传感器监视
4 参数/速度设置
5 打印当前或上次结果
Z 捕获局部原点
T 开关250ms遥测
X 紧急停车并使原点失效
H 重印菜单
```

平移菜单方向为：`1前、2右前、3右、4右后、5后、6左后、7左、8左前`；
`G` 循环1到4格，`M` 循环M1到M3，`V` 进设置，`Z` 取原点，`R` 启动，
`P` 打印结果，`B` 返回。

旋转菜单：`1/2/3` 选择90/180/360度，`D` 切换CW/CCW，`M` 切换
IMU/ENCODER停止源，`V/Z/R/P/B` 含义同上。

设置菜单：`1/2/3` 选择速度100/120/150，`G` 改格数，`M` 改M1到M3，
`P` 打印物理常数。传感器菜单可用 `Z` 清零，然后手转单轮一圈或手转车体90度；
`P` 单次输出，`T` 开关250ms连续输出。

关键遥测：

```text
POINT_STATUS：状态、故障、原点、目标、传感器模式、进度、剩余量、耗时
POINT_POSE：编码器/视觉局部前右位移、沿程/横向位移、IMU/编码器角度、三轮累计值
POINT_CTRL：目标速度、三轮目标/实测/PWM、UART队列
PID_WHEEL / PID_YAW：原有各级PID中间量与饱和状态
```

## 2026-07-17：流程1编码器结果与3秒紧凑遥测（当前最新）

用户按“后轮、左前、右前”顺序，在菜单3内每次Z清零后手转物理轮一圈。
`7100873`日志最终稳定值为：

```text
后轮：wheel_count[R,LF,RF] = (约4270, 0, 0)
左前：wheel_count[R,LF,RF] = (0, 约4264, 0)
右前：wheel_count[R,LF,RF] = (0, 0, 约4488)
```

结论：三只物理轮与三个软件通道映射正确，没有串路；本次手转方向下三路均为正。
左前没有显著少于后轮，后/左差小于1%；右前比二者高约5%。更重要的是，
三路一圈均明显接近4.3k而非代码当前假设的2048，可能来源于编码器四倍频定义、
铭牌PPR/CPR口径或实际传动比。当前先不改 `WHEEL_CPR=2048`，下一步应将每只轮
标记起点并慢速、准确旋转整整一圈后按P取最终值，至少重复两次；若仍稳定在约
4.3k，再根据三轮平均值或分轮标定策略修改距离换算。

## 2026-07-17：每轮两次精确一圈复测，确认有效CPR应为4096（尚未改代码）

用户使用 `7100874`，按后轮、左前、右前顺序，每轮各做两次Z清零后一圈测试。

```text
后轮：4290、4439
左前：4096、4276（第二次结束后准备下一轮时RF还带入346，不计入LF本体）
右前：4084、4104
```

映射仍完全正确，主要通道外只有手动操作/换轮产生的极少附带脉冲。右前两次相对
4096仅约-0.29%和+0.20%，左前一次正好4096；其余值只向偏大方向偏离，符合手动
越过一圈标记的特征。因此应采用编码器标准有效值 `4096 count/rev`，不应把人工
过转数据平均后写成4215左右。

当前代码仍是 `WHEEL_CPR=2048`，本次仅完成分析，尚未修改。若按4096为真实值，
当前 `mm/count` 被放大2倍，自动平移会在实际约100mm时误判已经走完200mm一格。
下一步应经用户确认后，把运动控制全局距离换算改为4096，再编译新识别号，然后
先做M1一格前进的卷尺实测；轮间剩余差异应通过实际直线/独立轮标定再决定是否需要
分轮比例，不能直接由人工一圈偏差写成三个不同CPR。

## 2026-07-17：CPR4096 后速度100一格前进实测

固件识别号为 `7100875`。M1、速度100、前进1格启动后，车体自身移动不足1cm；随后人工推动约12cm，过程可能打滑。日志显示控制状态机正常进入 `RUNNING`，目标距离200mm，PWM已经输出，但约3秒后里程只累计23mm，之后编码器数据基本不再变化，并在10秒时触发 `TIMEOUT`。

关键判断：此次结果不能用于校准CPR。人工推动期间编码器主要只有左前轮累计约950 count，后轮约-38、右前轮约7；真实的整车前进120mm不应呈现这种单轮占绝对多数的分布，说明存在轮胎打滑、单轮滚动或其余轮未随车转动。CPR4096的编译与运行配置已经正确生效，当前更像速度100低于整车静摩擦起步区间，而不是CPR错误。

下一次保持同一固件，不改PID、不推车：选择速度150后重新执行M1前进1格。命令流程为 `B -> 4 -> 3 -> B -> 1 -> 1 -> Z -> R`。若1至2秒仍不动，先按 `P`保存状态，再按 `X`急停，不等10秒超时；若能运行到DONE，则测量同一车体参考点的实际位移，并记录直线偏差、打滑现象和完整日志。

## 2026-07-17：速度150两次均约36cm，确认停车阶段软件问题并修复

用户用固件7100875、M1、速度150、前进1格连续测试两次，实测均约36cm。两次日志高度一致：运行阶段分别在500ms和540ms时到达202.3mm、207.3mm，证明CPR4096和运动距离换算已经基本正确；随后进入500ms `SETTLING`，编码器里程却继续增加到391.3mm、397.9mm。

根因位于 `point_begin_settle()`：`motion_heading_lock_stop()`只把速度目标归零并保留运行期累计的位置目标。运行到200mm时，左右轮实际位置约为正负4000 count，但位置目标已累计到约正负13000 count，位置误差被钳位到正负3000，位置补偿达到正负50，停车阶段仍输出约正负2000 PWM，因此又继续行驶接近一格。这不是CPR比例造成的36cm。

修复前备份：
```text
backup/mcu_before_point_settle_rebase_7100875_20260717
```

MCU识别号更新为 `7100876`，阶段标记：
```text
#MCU_BOOT id=7100876 stage=POINT_SETTLE_REBASE_CPR4096 safe=1 debug=2 display=OFF
```

修复内容仅在平移测试进入SETTLING时，在 `motion_heading_lock_stop()` 后调用 `motion_heading_lock_rebase_position()`，将三轮位置保持目标重定位到制动瞬间的实测位置，清除不可达的旧累计目标。未修改CPR、轮径、PID参数、速度档位、500ms停车时间、三轮映射、方向、视觉代码或赛道链路。

## 2026-07-18：7100882实测总账、视觉延迟结论与后续唯一主线

本节覆盖上一节末尾“继续测试M3视觉停车”的建议。实车已经证明该建议不再适用，后续不得重复做已经完成的基础测试。

### 一、当前不可再改的控制基线

- 编码器真实CPR为4096，轮径58mm，`ODOMETRY_QUICK_SCALE=0.915` 已由启动行 `odom_scale_x1000=915` 证明真实生效。
- 当前点测固件为 `#MCU_BOOT id=7100882 stage=GRID_VIS_CONFIRM safe=1 debug=2 display=OFF`。
- 当前测试速度统一以120为主，固定制动提前量50mm，停车保持500ms。
- 先前讨论过的前/后/左/右独立制动提前量都只是不同速度、不同距离样本下的候选拟合，尚未写入代码，也不是最终参数；视觉融合状态机验证前不再改这些值，避免同时引入两个变量。
- M1=`ENC_STOP+IMU_HOLD`；M2=`ENC_STOP+YAW_OFF`；M3=`VIS_STOP+IMU_HOLD`。
- 实测距离与M1编码器DONE里程高度一致，因此不得再次修改全局CPR或0.915比例；剩余误差属于方向相关的停车/机械动态。

### 二、已完成测试总账（不得无故重复）

1. 7100882、速度120、M3、前进一格共3次：实测52.0、54.6、54.0cm，并有向右偏。日志中编码器实际约530至541mm，视觉到160mm附近时车辆已经约474至487mm，随后均出现 `GRID_MISMATCH`。结论：M3在运动中直接依赖延迟视觉刹停必然严重超调，M3方案判定失败，不再重复。
2. 7100882、速度120、M2、前进一格共3次：实测20.0、20.4、19.5cm。该组只用于关闭航向保持的对照，不作为正式方案，不再重复。
3. 7100882、速度120、M1、前进一格已有4个有效实测：20.0、19.4、20.0、19.1cm；M1前进单格基础能力已验证，不再重复做普通距离标定。
4. 7100882、速度120、M1、连续两格已有完整四方向结果：前进41.4、41.2、40.0cm；后退41.9、40.0cm；右移39.0、38.4cm；左移38.0、37.9cm。对应编码器终点与尺量一致。结论：前后略长、左右偏短，且左移最短；不再重复普通两格标定。
5. 7100882、速度120、M1、单格补测：后退20.1cm（编码器196.0mm）；左移18.6cm（编码器182.6mm）；右移19.0cm（编码器约183.8mm）。结论：单格后退合格，左右偏短是真实制动结果，不是视觉测量误差。
6. 7100882、速度150、M1、两格的后/左/右样本约为40.0、39.9、42.0cm。该组速度不同，只保留作速度相关制动证据，严禁与速度120数据混算。
7. 三格前进中86.2cm样本已因启动航向异常判定无效；另外两次62.5cm有效。结论是连续多格固定制动会积累误差，当前方向确定为逐格停车，不再重复连续三格标定。

### 三、视觉延迟已被直接实测证明

以下全部是M1编码器停车、视觉仅旁路观察：

- 前进一格实际19.1cm：DONE时视觉约10cm，随后打印才追到18cm。
- 前进两格实际41.4cm：DONE时视觉约25.9cm，随后依次追到33.9cm和39.9cm。
- 后退一格实际20.1cm：DONE时视觉约4cm，稍后追到19.8cm。
- 左移一格实际18.6cm：DONE时视觉约5.9cm，稍后追到16.1cm。
- 右移一格实际19.0cm：DONE时视觉约5.8cm，稍后追到18.0cm。

因此 `vision_link` 的 `age=5~50ms` 只代表“MCU最近收到一个串口包”，不代表该包描述的是5~50ms前的真实车辆状态。端到端链路是全局摄像头→电脑→手机屏幕→车载OpenART→MCU，包到达时仍可能是数百毫秒前的画面。只统计“停车后收到3个新包”不能排除旧画面。

### 四、7100882当前实现与目标方案的差距

- 点测M3在RUNNING阶段把 `vision_along_mm` 直接作为进度并决定刹车，已经实测失败。
- 点测M1虽然运动正确，但SETTLING满500ms后直接DONE，完全没有执行视觉确认，所以M1日志中的 `POINT_VIS_CONFIRM frames=0/3` 是正常现象，不能把这些测试称为“视觉确认已通过”。
- 正式赛道 `action_follower` 仍在运动阶段用收到的视觉坐标实时计算目标距离、方向和减速点；只有它认为到达70mm范围后才停车500ms并计3个新包。由于视觉延迟，它并不是真正的“编码器先走一格”。
- 当前3帧确认只看位置包计数递增，没有画面采集时间戳，也没有视觉位置稳定性判断，可能把仍在管线中的旧帧当成停车后新观测。

### 五、后续唯一主线（未实现，下一步应先修改代码）

目标改为“停走式视觉融合”，禁止视觉参与单格运动中的实时刹车：

1. 每步开始时保存已经稳定的视觉绝对位置、计划中的下一格坐标和IMU航向基准。
2. 运动阶段仅由编码器累计200mm（以后可按视觉残差修正为约180~220mm）并由IMU保持航向；忽略运动中到达的延迟视觉位置，不用视觉改刹车点，也不连续用视觉角度修正IMU。
3. 编码器到位后速度归零，保持当前位置和航向，进入独立的 `VIS_DRAIN/VIS_STABLE` 阶段。
4. 视觉确认不能只数新包：先等待最小排空时间，再要求视觉位置连续多帧落在小范围内且帧号持续前进，证明旧运动画面已经排空、位置已经稳定。
5. 稳定后比较视觉绝对位置与计划格心：小偏差只重置融合位姿；中等偏差用于修正下一格的编码器目标距离；过大偏差锁车并报告，不盲目继续。
6. 每一格都重复“编码器+IMU走格→停车→视觉稳定→比较/重定位→下一格”。最终Action结束也必须完成视觉稳定确认。
7. 首先在点测模式增加独立混合模式验证上述状态机；通过后再移植到 `action_follower`。后续实车只测这个新模式，不再重复M1/M2/M3基础距离测试。

当前首要目标不是继续微调四方向距离，也不是再次测试M3，而是先实现并验证上述“编码器逐格 + 停车后视觉稳定确认”通路。

## 2026-07-18：7100883延迟视觉M4已实现，等待实车验证

本轮严格沿上一节唯一主线实施，新增点测M4，未修改正式赛道 `action_follower`。修改前同时备份MCU、视觉端、设计文档、工程配置和7100882 AXF：

```text
backup/checkpoint_7100882_before_delayed_vision_m4_20260718
```

备份目录内 `BACKUP_MANIFEST.txt` 保存了关键文件SHA-256。视觉端 `openart/新视觉main.py` 修改前后SHA-256均为：

```text
7D8D6E67A6D65CD5DA623793986BAEFB90A32D7BB33DF808905FF7AACCB62C15
```

因此本轮视觉端未更新、无需重新拷入OpenART；仅MCU端更新。

### 一、M4含义和状态机

菜单增加M4=`ENC_MOVE+DELAY_VIS_CHECK`，仅用于点测。M1/M2/M3行为保持原样，尤其M3仍是已经判定失败的旧式运动中视觉停车，不再使用。

M4执行顺序固定为：

```text
RUNNING(编码器测距+IMU航向保持)
-> SETTLING(速度归零并位置保持500ms)
-> VIS_DRAIN(1000ms，只吸收并丢弃视觉在途帧)
-> VIS_STABLE(等待稳定视觉位置)
-> DONE或FAULT
```

运动阶段的进度、减速和50mm制动提前量全部只读编码器，视觉不参与运动中停车。视觉掉线也不会使运动中途切换控制源；停车后会等待视觉恢复，直到总确认超时。

稳定判断不再使用“包计数一次跳多少就算多少帧”。每个不同 `frame_id` 最多计1帧，要求连续6个不同帧的位置都在以首帧为中心的±0.1格窗口内（约20mm），并且稳定持续至少200ms。若先稳定在延迟链路中的旧位置，只增加一次 `bad_clusters` 并继续等待，不立刻锁车；从开始停车起总等待上限6秒。最终稳定位置相对目标的纵向和横向误差均不超过60mm则DONE，否则持续等更新，超时后以 `GRID_MISMATCH` 或 `VIS_CONFIRM_TIMEOUT` 报告。

本版只完成“停车后稳定视觉比较/诊断”，不会在视觉误差出现后再次驱动车轮，也尚未把M4移植到正式赛道 `action_follower`。必须先用本节唯一流程验证状态机，再决定视觉重定位和下一格距离补偿。

### 二、诊断输出

新增状态：

```text
VIS_DRAIN
VIS_STABLE
```

新增/替换串口行：

```text
POINT_VIS_PIPE phase=... frames=.../6 bad_clusters=... frame=... age=... pos=... drain=... vis_err=(along,cross)mm vis-enc=(along,cross)mm policy=...
```

- `drain`：剩余排空时间。
- `frames`：当前稳定簇已累计的唯一视觉帧数。
- `bad_clusters`：稳定过但尚未落在目标附近的旧位置/错误位置簇数量。
- `vis_err`：稳定视觉位置相对计划目标的纵向、横向误差。
- `vis-enc`：视觉相对编码器最终里程的纵向、横向差值。

识别号：

```text
#MCU_BOOT id=7100883 stage=DELAYED_VIS_M4 safe=1 debug=2 display=OFF
UART8_CONFIG ... m4=ENC_IMU_MOVE+HOLD500+DRAIN1000+STABLE6_200MS+VIS_CHECK60
```

### 三、构建与差异审计

0U、1U、2U均使用Keil `-r` 全量重建，均为0 Error、0 Warning；最终恢复：

```text
#define CONTROL_DEBUG_ENABLE (2U)
```

最终AXF：

```text
project/mdk/Objects/rt1064.axf
SHA256: 384A0DEA6E1F37BD11A8CE77196CAE4ED28AA18021A6371140C7AECF7AEC4F5E
```

完成后的可恢复检查点：

```text
backup/checkpoint_7100883_delayed_visual_m4_20260718
```

相对7100882备份，MCU业务源码只改动 `point_test.c`、`point_test.h`、`pc_console.c`。`motion_control.c`、所有PID、CPR4096、轮径58mm、里程比例0.915、速度档位、固定50mm制动提前量、三轮映射与方向均未修改。正式赛道0U只是因公共点测代码和识别号重新构建，赛道跟随逻辑未迁移M4。

### 四、下一次唯一实车流程（每次从重新上电开始）

1. 摄像头和MCU一起上电，先保持车完全静止至少3秒，让视觉链路中的旧画面排空。
2. 串口确认出现 `id=7100883`、`stage=DELAYED_VIS_M4`、`VISION CONNECTED`。
3. 只做速度120、M4、前进一格，完整菜单序列：

```text
4 -> 2 -> M -> M -> M -> B -> 1 -> 1 -> Z -> R
```

4. `Z` 前仍需保持车不动；`R` 后不要推车，不要按P，不要发送其他命令，等待自动DONE或FAULT。
5. 正常状态顺序应为 `RUNNING -> SETTLING -> VIS_DRAIN -> VIS_STABLE -> DONE`。保存从启动行到最终菜单的完整串口文本，并测量实际位移一次。
6. 若菜单显示的传感器不是 `ENC_MOVE+DELAY_VIS_CHECK`，不要运行；按M直到M4。不得再测试M3。

下一轮只分析这一次M4前进单格的状态转换、`bad_clusters`、`frames`、`vis_err` 和 `vis-enc`；不重复既有M1/M2/M3距离测试。
